# Changelog

All notable changes to ИСПУМ. Newest first. Dates are when the change **shipped to
production**. Work that's built but not yet deployed lives under **[Unreleased]**.

This is the *internal* engineering log. The user-facing, curated changelog is the
public **Обновления платформы** page (`frontend/src/pages/Changelog.tsx`) — promote
highlights there when you want users to know.

Format loosely follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com): grouped into
**Added / Changed / Fixed**.

---

## [Unreleased]

### Fixed
- **Live QR quiz — students on some networks couldn't join or got kicked out mid-test.** Reported after a real in-class run (6 students): some couldn't connect at all, some needed a VPN and others didn't, and even connected students hit errors once the quiz started. Root-caused to two things, neither purely an ISP issue:

  1. **The only foreign, render-blocking dependency in the app's load path was `fonts.googleapis.com`** (`index.html`'s Google Fonts `<link>`). Google domains are inconsistently throttled/blocked on some Russian ISPs/mobile carriers — a student on such a network would sit on a blank page waiting for a request that never resolves, while a VPN (different egress, routes around whatever's blocking Google specifically) fixed it. Self-hosted both families instead: fetched the exact same subsets the CSS2 API was already serving from `fonts.gstatic.com` (`frontend/public/fonts/*.woff2`, DM Sans has no Cyrillic cut upstream — that was already true, unchanged) into local `@font-face` rules in `src/index.css`, dropped the `<link>`, and tightened `deploy/nginx/gradeassist.conf`'s CSP (`style-src`/`font-src` no longer need to allow-list Google).
  2. **`LiveJoin.tsx`'s student-side poll loop died permanently on the first failed request** — a single timeout, dropped packet, or transient 5xx/429 (exactly what venue/mobile wifi produces) set a terminal "Сессия недоступна" with no retry and no recovery short of reloading (which also silently rejoined as a *new* participant, since the token only lived in React state — risking an orphaned in-progress score). Now retries transient failures with backoff (6 attempts, 1s→8s) before surfacing an error, while a genuine 404 (session/participant actually gone) still fails fast instead of retrying pointlessly; `participant_token` persists to `sessionStorage` so an accidental reload resumes instead of rejoining; `join()` itself gets the same fast-fail-on-404/retry-on-blip treatment instead of a single-shot "проверьте код" that blamed the join code for what was often just a network hiccup.

  Verified: both self-hosted font requests confirmed in the network tab (no `fonts.googleapis.com` traffic), a live join attempt against a real backend correctly fast-failed with "Сессия не найдена" on a 404 rather than retrying, `tsc`/build/full test suite clean.
- **"Сохранить в журнал" (save live-quiz results to the gradebook) was styled as the muted secondary button, with "К тестам" (exit, no consequence) right below it in bold primary amber** — the low-stakes action visually outranked the one that actually matters, on both the self-paced live view and the "Сессия завершена" leaderboard screen ([LiveSessionHost.tsx](frontend/src/pages/LiveSessionHost.tsx)). Swapped: save now defaults to `primary`, exit is explicit `secondary`.

### Added
- **Презентации — план лекции показывается до генерации (TODO.md "### AO" Phase 0).** Generation has run as outline + parallel expansion since AG Phase 1, but the outline was invisible: the teacher waited minutes behind a spinner and then got a finished deck whose structure they could only change by regenerating the whole thing (new job, new spend, everything they liked rerolled) or by editing it in PowerPoint — which forks the deck out of ИСПУМ permanently. The two halves are now separable and the teacher sits between them.

  `services/presentations.ts`'s `generatePresentation` split into `planPresentation()` (one LLM call: slide count/order/type + a one-line brief each — no RAG, no DB writes) and `expandPresentation()` (the ~20-call half: per-batch retrieval, writing, images, persistence); `generatePresentation()` is now just the two composed, so the legacy sync route and `evalPresentations.ts` are untouched. `presentationJobWorker.ts` gains a `stage` discriminator on the payload — `'outline'` parks the job at the new `outline_ready` status, `'expand'` writes the deck from the outline stored on the row, `'full'` (and any pre-gate message with no stage) is the old back-to-back behaviour. Web-search grounding is captured during the plan pass and replayed into expansion, so pausing for approval costs no extra search call; RAG scope is deliberately *re*-resolved at expansion instead (a course whose sharing changed while the plan sat should be honoured as it is now).

  Frontend: `OutlineEditor.tsx` (new) edits structure only — title, type, order, insert, delete — and «Написать слайды» spends the rest. Slide *content* editing is Phase 1; a body editor here would ask the teacher to write the deck themselves, which is the opposite of the point. The poll loop starts fast (1.2s) and relaxes to 2.5s, since the plan lands in seconds where the deck takes minutes. The gate is **opt-out, not opt-in** («Показать план лекции перед генерацией», on by default) — but the backend reads a *missing* `review_outline` as `true` and the client always sends it explicitly, so a cached pre-gate bundle can't land in a gate it has no UI for.

  Quota semantics kept honest: the monthly limit is still *checked* at enqueue, but usage is billed in `expandPresentation` — an outline abandoned at the gate created no deck and must not count. Every state transition is a conditional `UPDATE` rather than read-then-write: two «Продолжить» clicks racing would otherwise enqueue two expansions of one job (two decks, two bills), and a worker retry could otherwise replace a plan the teacher is already editing. Unconfirmed plans expire after 24h through `scheduleWithLease` (invariant 11), clearing the stored `params` with them — an abandoned draft is no reason to hold 20k chars of someone's conspectus indefinitely.

  Migration 118 is additive-only and `status` has no CHECK constraint, so the previous release runs unchanged against this schema (invariant 12). 13 new tests (worker stages + `normaliseEditedOutline`, which deliberately does *not* repair structure the way `normaliseOutline` does — a teacher who deleted the title slide meant to); full suite 969 green. Gate queries verified end to end against a real database — park, re-park refused, confirm, double-confirm refused, sweep, params cleared.

- **Кафедральная библиотека (TODO.md "### AN") — document RAG gets a scope ladder, plus a drawing library.** Two gaps prompted this: some disciplines (mechanical engineering especially) hit real limits in the model's knowledge of ГОСТ/ЕСКД conventions and specific drawings, and teachers' own accumulated materials had no way to reach beyond their own course. `document_chunks`/`documents` were hard-scoped to `course_id` — deliberately left that way by migration 092, whose own comment names this exact widening as future work. That widening is now built: `visibility_scope` (`course`/`unit`/`institution`/`platform`) + `provenance` on `documents`, denormalized onto `document_chunks` (migration 116). Retrieval (`findRelevantChunks`/`findRelevantChunksScored` in `db/queries/chunks.ts`, now scope-aware via the new `services/ragScope.ts`) always fills a course's own material first, then tops up remaining slots from pooled кафедра/institution/platform scope — own uploads are never displaced. Promotion (`PATCH /api/documents/:id/scope`) reuses the existing org-tree domain grants (`umu`:edit on the target unit, or institution-root + the new `documentLibraryInstitution` plan flag for institution-wide) rather than inventing a moderation queue; `scope_unit_id` defaults to the teacher's own department so the common case needs no unit picker. Reuse is logged (`rag_document_uses`) and surfaced both to the institution (`GET /api/institution/library`, new «Библиотека кафедры» page, `umu` domain) and back to the contributor.

  **Figure library (migration 117, `document_figures`)** — a material's embedded/scanned drawings are now persisted (object storage) instead of being OCR'd for text and discarded, addressing the actual "drawings" half of the original idea. Two extraction paths: `.docx` embedded images (`documentExtractor.ts`'s `extractDocxFigures`, scans `word/media/*`) and PDF (`extractPdfFigures`) — the more load-bearing one in practice, since a separately-scanned чертёж/схема is far more often a standalone PDF than a Word-embedded image. PDF pages are selected by the same per-page real-word signal the existing "[стр. N]" citation convention already relies on (`\f`-split text) — a text-sparse page is treated as image-dominated and rasterized (`yandexVision.ts`'s `rasterizePdfPages`, now exported), capped at 80 scanned pages/20 stored figures per document to bound worst-case cost on a huge upload. Captioning deliberately does **not** use a multimodal model — none of this codebase's chat providers accept image content (`ChatMessage.content` is `string`-only across deepseek/yandex/qwen) — so a figure is captioned from what Yandex Vision OCR reads off the sheet itself (a чертёж's title block is designed to carry exactly this: ГОСТ/номер, наименование, обозначения) plus that page's own surrounding text, via a plain `chatJSON` call. `services/presentations.ts`'s `autoFillImages` now checks the figure library before falling back to `yandexImageSearch`, so a matching library drawing is preferred over a generic web photo.

  **True VLM captioning — built as a follow-up the same day.** DeepSeek shipped an experimental vision-capable model (`deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp`, released 2026-08-21, confirmed against api-docs.deepseek.com/guides/vision) — the architecturally consistent choice flagged above, since DeepSeek (not Qwen, which `qwen.ts`'s own doc comment excludes from every institution-facing path for sovereignty reasons) is the platform's actual default/institution-facing provider. `services/llm/deepseek.ts` gains `captionImage()` — a standalone method outside the `LLMProvider` interface (same "always this one provider, no per-institution routing" shape `embed()` already has for Yandex), reusing the *existing* multi-account/cooldown/retry/Telegram-alert infrastructure `chat()` already has rather than a parallel bespoke implementation — the DeepSeek-specific delta turned out to be small once that infrastructure was there to reuse. `services/llm/types.ts` gains `VisionContentPart` (text/image_url parts), deliberately **not** folded into the shared `ChatMessage.content` (kept `string`-only) — `yandex.ts` builds its own `{role, text}` request straight off `m.content` as a string, and widening the shared type would force every provider file to defensively handle a case only DeepSeek can serve. `figureCaptioning.ts`'s `captionFigure` now tries vision first (when an image is supplied and the feature is enabled) and falls back to the existing OCR+text `chatJSON` path on any failure — vision never throws, so "disabled", "unavailable", and "the call itself failed" are all indistinguishable to the caller, all degrading the same way.

  **Gated behind `DEEPSEEK_VISION_ENABLED` (default off, explicit opt-in)** — unlike the rest of this codebase's auto-enabled best-effort calls, because it costs real money per figure *and* the vendor itself labels the endpoint experimental (shape/availability could change). Billed at FLASH's per-token rate (images count as up to 384 input tokens each) — no new cost-rate table needed, `calculateDeepSeekCost(..., 'deepseek-v4-flash')` is reused as-is. 24 new tests in `deepseek.test.ts` (disabled-by-default, success, multi-account fallback, non-retryable-fails-fast, malformed-response degrades to null, usage-log shape) plus 10 in the new `figureCaptioning.test.ts` covering the vision-first/OCR-fallback dispatch.

  `backend/migrations/116_document_scope_and_provenance.sql`, `117_document_figures.sql`; `services/ragScope.ts`, `figureCaptioning.ts`; `db/queries/ragDocumentUses.ts`, `documentFigures.ts`; `routes/documents.ts` (`PATCH /:id/scope`, `GET /:id/reuse`, `GET /figures/:id/image`), `routes/institution.ts` (`GET /library`); `pages/institution/InstitutionLibrary.tsx`, `components/ui/DocumentUpload.tsx` (inline «Поделиться с кафедрой»). 5 new integration tests for the scope ladder in `chunks.integration.test.ts`.

  **The missing upload surface, caught by "do we have UI for uploading course content?"** All of the above assumed a teacher could upload a `document_type='material'` document to a course — the backend has always supported it (unlimited materials per course, chunked/embedded for RAG) — but no frontend page ever did: `components/ui/DocumentUpload.tsx` was only wired to the single syllabus slot (`pages/Courses.tsx`) and the grading submission uploader (`components/grading/GradingForm.tsx`). A teacher could share their syllabus but never the чертёж collection the whole feature exists for. Fixed with a proper "Материалы предмета" panel (`components/courses/CourseMaterials.tsx`, new) on the course edit view: lists a teacher's own materials for the course, an upload dropzone that remounts after each success (multiple materials, not one), delete, and the same «Поделиться с кафедрой» control per listed item (`DocumentUpload.tsx`'s `SharePrompt` exported and reused rather than duplicated). Backend gains the two query functions this needed — `listDocumentsForCourse` (owner-scoped; a colleague's shared materials surface separately via the institution library, never mixed in here) and `deleteDocumentOwnedByTeacher` (cascades to `document_chunks`/`document_figures` via their existing FKs, best-effort object-storage cleanup) — behind `GET /api/documents?course_id=&document_type=` and `DELETE /api/documents/:id`. Also added a revoke control (`«Вернуть только предмету»`) to the already-shared state, which the backend's demote-to-`'course'` path already supported but nothing called. 5 new integration tests in `documents.materials.integration.test.ts`.

- **Rename/delete an институт group in Мониторинг РПД.** The backend already had `PATCH`/`DELETE /api/institution/rpd/mapping/groups/:id`, but no frontend ever called them — a mistyped institute name (e.g. a typo made while grouping кафедры by hand) had no self-service fix. Added a "Институты" list to `RpdMonitor.tsx` with inline rename and delete (delete cascades via `rpd_dept_group_members.group_id ON DELETE CASCADE`, so the group's кафедры fall back to "без института" rather than being orphaned).

### Fixed
- **ФОС structural check no longer requires two sections a ФОС structurally cannot have yet.** Flagged by the методист testing the new макет conformance check: it reported «Рассмотрение на заседании кафедры» and «Гриф УТВЕРЖДЕНО» as missing on a real ФОС, but both are filled in by a downstream signing workflow — the кафедра meeting that assigns a protocol number, then the УМЦ/зав. магистратурой sign-off — that happens *after* a ФОС is reviewed here, not before. Same false-positive shape СОГЛАСОВАНО was already excluded for, just missed the first time: checking for text that cannot exist yet at authoring time would fire on every ФОС this check is ever run against.

  `services/fosStructure.ts`'s required-section list drops from 8 to 6 (`department_minutes`, `approved` removed from `FosSectionKey`); the export/generator still print the blank-template lines for both, since a кафедра does eventually fill them in outside ИСПУМ — only the *check* stopped requiring them. 1 new regression test, 75 backend tests in the affected files green.

### Added
- **§9 БРС readiness — the last link in the chain, checked at authoring time.** A ФОС's перечень *is* its discipline's §9, so a §9 that is incomplete or doesn't total 60/100 cannot produce a conformant ФОС however carefully the ФОС is written. `services/brsReadiness.ts` answers «можно ли из этого п.9 собрать корректный ФОС» — deterministic (arithmetic and list membership), and it runs whether or not a ФОС exists yet, because the point is to catch it while the teacher is still writing rather than when a методист reviews.

  Blocks on: a row missing its min or max, a min above its max, a semester not totalling 60/100. Warns (never blocks) on an instrument the макет's catalogue doesn't describe — a кафедра may legitimately use one, it just means the ФОС needs a hand-written «краткая характеристика». A semester containing an incomplete row is skipped for the total rule, so one gap yields one finding rather than two.

  **РПД студия didn't produce a §9 at all** — it drafts four sections, none of them the БРС table, so there was nothing to validate. It now drafts one, and drafts it *correct*: the model names the instruments, `allocateBrsPoints` assigns the numbers so each semester totals exactly 60/100, with промежуточная аттестация weighted at the макет's own share (экзамен 24/40 of 60/100). Models don't reliably make columns add up, and a §9 that misses its total is precisely what blocks a conformant ФОС downstream — so the numbers are never the model's to choose. A test reads the drafted table back and runs the readiness check on it.

  **The tests caught a false positive that would have fired on nearly every РПД in the university:** «Экзамен» is absent from the макет's instrument catalogue *by design* — промежуточная аттестация gets an экзаменационный билет there, not a «краткая характеристика» row — so checking it against that list flagged it as unlisted. Экзамен/зачёт are now exempt, and `FINAL_ATTESTATION` moved to `config/brs.ts` so `assessmentLinkage.ts`, which already exempted them from СРС/КСР for the same underlying reason, shares one definition.

  Also: `BRS_SEMESTER_MIN/MAX` moved out of `assessmentLinkage.ts` into `config/brs.ts` — a third consumer appeared, and wiring the readiness check in would otherwise have created another circular import (the second one this feature has produced; the first was `fosStructure`). They are institution POLICY rather than a fact about Russian higher education, so `config/` is where a second institution would override them.

  16 new tests; 934 backend / 61 frontend green, both builds clean.

### Added
- **The ФОС generator now emits the «Макет ФОС 3++» shape — conformant by construction.** Closes the loop opened by the методист's 2026-08-25 request: instead of generating a pile of instruments and then checking whether it matches the макет, the generator produces the макет's own skeleton, and the checks become regressions for hand-written ФОС.

  The pivot is where the numbers come from. The макет says «перечень оценочных средств приводиться из п.9 рабочей программы», so the generator now parses §9 out of the course's own syllabus **with the same `parseAssessmentLinkage` the linkage check uses**. Same parser, same numbers — the ФОС↔§9 reconciliation has nothing to report because both sides read one source.

  Ingested the макет's two normative tables as reference data (`services/fosMacketReference.ts`): the «Шкала оценивания» (5/4/3/2 with their балл ranges) and the 28-row «Краткая характеристика оценочных средств» catalogue, trimmed per document to the instruments a discipline actually uses. Instruments the макет doesn't list are skipped rather than described from thin air. Deliberately **not** ingested: the per-instrument point breakdowns, which are «например» illustrations each кафедра sets for itself — baking them in would turn one department's example into everyone's requirement.

  **Criteria points are assigned in code, never by the model.** `buildInstrumentCriteria` asks only for the wording — what each part of a mark is awarded for — then `distributePoints` splits §9's min/max across those parts so they sum back exactly. Making numbers add up is the one thing a model is worst at, and those sums are precisely what the conformance check verifies.

  **The claim is tested, not asserted.** `fosExport.test.ts` builds a document, renders the DOCX, reads the text back with mammoth the way `documentExtractor` would, and runs `checkFosStructure` and `checkFosScores` on it — the same checks a методист would. Zero findings, all 8 sections present. Verified non-vacuous by deleting a required section from the export (test goes red) and by breaking `distributePoints` (two tests go red).

  That last check earned its keep immediately: `tsc` caught the test passing the generator's `FosInstrumentCriteria` (itemised components) where `checkFosScores` expects `FosCriteriaBlock` (pre-summed), leaving `component_min/max` undefined — so **the sum rules were being skipped and the assertion passed vacuously**. The test now collapses components the way `parseFosNumbers` would when reading the rendered document back.

  «СОГЛАСОВАНО» is deliberately not printed (the макет includes it only for kafedras writing a ФОС for another kafedra), and institutional fields the generator can't know — факультет, кафедра, направление, профиль — print the макет's own blank rules rather than a guess. Legacy ФОС generated before this still render, with a test pinning it. 918 backend / 61 frontend green.

### Added
- **Per-instrument «Критерии оценки» sums — the third link in the ФОС chain.** Completes §9 → перечень ФОС → критерии оценки. The макет's own лабораторная criteria table sums to 12/20, exactly its перечень row; two new checks pin that: a block's itemised parts must add up to the total it declares, and that declared total must match its перечень row.

  **Extraction had to go to the model, and the reason is specific.** The макет writes criteria two ways — as a table (Виды работ | Минимальный балл | Максимальный балл | ИТОГО) and as prose («максимальная оценка за работу составляет 20 баллов, минимальная 10. Из них: Презентация работы – мах 3 балла; …») — and the prose interleaves the declared total with its own components. Regex-summing every «мах N баллов» would count the total twice and report a mismatch on a correct document. So the model separates total from components (told explicitly not to include the total among them) and the arithmetic stays in code, same split as the rest of the file.

  Folded into the existing ФОС pass rather than adding a third LLM call — `parseFosScoreTable` became `parseFosNumbers`, returning both the перечень rows and the criteria blocks from one read of the same document.

  Three cases deliberately produce nothing rather than a guess: a block that was never itemised (an empty component list is not a sum of zero), a block that declares no total of its own, and an instrument scored differently per semester — a criteria block is written once, so there is no single перечень total to compare it against. Each has a test.

  The side-by-side numbers box now labels its two columns by finding kind: «п.9 РПД / ФОС» is right for the reconciliation kinds but wrong for the criteria ones, where both numbers come from inside the ФОС («Заявлено в критериях» / «Сумма составляющих»). 10 new tests built on the макет's worked numbers; 908 backend / 61 frontend green.

  **Remaining on ФОС conformance:** making the ФОС generator emit the макет shape, so conformance is true by construction rather than checked after the fact — with РПД студия validating §9 against what the ФОС will later require.

### Added
- **ФОС structural conformance to «Макет ФОС 3++».** Second half of the 2026-08-25 request — «Хотелось бы, чтобы программа анализировала также правильность оформления в ФОСе по макету». New `services/fosStructure.ts`, deterministic (section presence is a string question — same call as the copy check, opposite of the meaning check, which genuinely needed a model).

  Checks the eight blocks the макет requires — титульный лист, составитель, протокол кафедры, гриф УТВЕРЖДЕНО, перечень компетенций/индикаторов с этапами формирования, перечень оценочных средств, шкала оценивания, краткая характеристика — and, using the §9 parse the linkage check already ran, that **every instrument the РПД budgets points for has a «Критерии оценки» block** in the fund.

  Three deliberate limits, all documented in the file header: it verifies section **presence, not content** (it knows «Шкала оценивания» exists, not whether its thresholds are right); it **never requires СОГЛАСОВАНО**, on the макет's own instruction that only kafedras writing a ФОС for another kafedra include it — requiring it would fire on the normal case; and it treats the макет's point breakdowns as the illustrations they are («например, максимальное количество баллов за деловую игру 20…»), since each кафедра sets its own per положение о БРС.

  **The макет is its own test fixture.** Flattened to text the way `documentExtractor` produces it and checked in as `__fixtures__/fosMacket.txt`, with the positive case asserting КНИТУ's own template passes its own check — if it ever fails, the check is wrong, not the document. That caught two real bugs immediately: `/критери\w* оценки/` never matched a single Cyrillic heading (**`\w` is ASCII-only in JavaScript**, the same trap as `\b` in the «Итого» guard the day before), and the instrument-to-criteria window only looked *backwards* — wrong for «Критерии оценки лабораторных работ», where the heading names its own instrument. Both found by the макет failing itself, not by inspection.

  Also fixed in passing: promoting `checkFosStructure` into `assessmentLinkage.ts` created a **circular import** (`fosStructure` → `assessmentLinkage` → `fosStructure`), which typechecks but can leave undefined bindings at runtime. `mentions`, the synonym splitter and `isTotalRow` moved down to `lib/ruText.ts` — the same promote-on-second-consumer call already made for `tokenContainment` — and `assessmentLinkage.ts` re-exports them so existing importers are untouched. 9 new tests; 899 backend / 61 frontend green.

  **Still open:** the third arithmetic rule the макет implies — per-instrument «Критерии оценки» sub-scores must sum to that instrument's Перечень row (the макет's лабораторная table sums to 12/20, matching its row) — and making the ФОС generator emit the макет shape so conformance is true by construction rather than checked after the fact.

### Added
- **ФОС «Перечень оценочных средств» ↔ п.9 РПД — the numbers, not just the names (methodist, 2026-08-25).** «программа видит похоже только наличие… Хотелось бы, чтобы программа анализировала также правильность оформления в ФОСе по макету… баллы должны брать из п.9 РП». She attached КНИТУ's «Макет ФОС 3++», which states the rule itself, printed directly under the table: *«Примечание: перечень оценочных средств приводиться из п.9 рабочей программы по дисциплине (модулю)»*. So this is arithmetic, not judgement — extraction goes to the model, reconciliation stays in code, same split as the rest of `assessmentLinkage.ts`.

  Three rules now checked: each instrument's **min and max** must match §9; nothing may appear on one side and not the other; and each semester must total **60/100** (confirmed as a hard КНИТУ БРС invariant — kept as a named constant with a note that a second institution should move it to settings rather than silently inherit КНИТУ's).

  **The blocker was that we were throwing the minimum away.** `brs_items` carried `points` (max only), as did the БРС engine's `BrsCheckpoint` — so the comparison the макет asks for was impossible until §9 parsing captured both columns. `brs_items` is now `BrsScoreRow { name, semester, min_points, max_points }`.

  **Per semester, not per discipline** — the thing that would have made a naive version wrong on every correct multi-semester РПД. Her Иностранный язык document repeats the same instrument with *different* points in each semester («Проект» 10/15, 10/15, 18/30), and 60/100 is a per-semester total; summing across three semesters gives 180/300 and flags everything. Matching never crosses a semester boundary, and there's a test pinning exactly that.

  Bug found while testing: **JavaScript's `\b` is ASCII-only**, so the `/(итого|всего)\b/` guard meant to exclude «Итого» rows never matched — Cyrillic «о» isn't a word character, so there's no boundary before the colon. Anchored to the whole cell instead, which also keeps a real instrument named «Итоговая аттестация» from being mistaken for a total. Caught by a test, not by inspection.

  Renders as its own section with both numbers side by side in tabular figures, so a mismatch is something the reader sees rather than takes on trust. 11 new backend tests built on the макет's worked example and her real document's numbers; 890 backend / 61 frontend green.

  **Not done, deliberately:** structural conformance to the макет (does the ФОС have титульный лист, шкала оценивания, краткая характеристика, per-instrument «Критерии оценки»…) and the third arithmetic rule the макет implies — that each instrument's «Критерии оценки» sub-scores sum to its Перечень row (лаб. criteria table sums to 12/20, matching its row). Also worth knowing: most point breakdowns in the макет are «например» illustrations, not norms — each кафедра sets its own per положение о БРС, so ingesting them as rules would manufacture false findings.

### Added
- **«Проверка смысла формулировок» — the judged half of the методист's ЗУВ rule.** Her follow-up once the copy check was fixed: «нужна еще проверка смысла, формулировки (то есть того, как формулировка "должен знать" отражает индикатор и есть ли смысловая связь с дисциплиной)». Copy-detection only answers *is this a literal duplicate*; a ЗУВ reworded just past the 0.9 containment threshold can still be generic boilerplate naming nothing this discipline teaches.

  New `services/outcomeMeaning.ts` — and **deliberately an LLM pass**, the opposite call from `outcomeFormulation.ts`, for the reason that file's header states from the other side: "is this text a copy of that text" is a measurable string question and belongs in code, while "does this wording convey that requirement's meaning, in terms this discipline actually teaches" is reading comprehension. Faking it with another similarity threshold would just be a worse copy detector. Per ЗУВ line it returns `not_reflected` (conveys no declared indicator) or `weak_link` (conveys it, but generically — the wording would suit any discipline); `ok` produces nothing. Judged against the §5–§8.1 content, so «связь с дисциплиной» is measured against real topics.

  Scoped to what the copy check leaves behind — a verbatim copy is already reported, and the model is **never even called** when every ЗУВ line is one (asserted: two LLM calls, not three). Findings are validated like coverage citations: a hallucinated `ref` is dropped, and an `indicator_code` the РПД never declared is nulled rather than echoed, so a finding never points at a row that doesn't exist.

  **Failure is not silence.** `checkOutcomeMeaning` throws on provider failure instead of returning `[]`, and `reviewSyllabus` records a `warnings` entry — an empty result has to keep meaning "checked, nothing wrong". That distinction is the whole lesson of the bug fixed alongside it, where a clean-looking empty result was a check doing nothing.

- **Double-counting is now stated rather than hidden.** A ЗУВ line that copies a requirement already in the list is the same requirement scored twice, inflating covered/partial/missing. New `duplicate_count`, surfaced in the verdict line and under the stat strip. Deliberately **not** silently subtracted: the three counts still sum to the cards on screen, and the reader is told how many are duplicates instead of finding numbers that don't reconcile with what they can see.

  Both new finding types render inline on the flagged card (same contract as the copy warning), with the standalone block kept as the overflow view for lines outside the `MAX_REQUIREMENTS` cap. Client timeouts on `syllabus-review` and `ad-hoc-review` raised 120s → 180s — the review is three sequential LLM passes now and 120s was sized for two, matching the repo's other multi-call endpoints. 16 new backend tests + 6 new render tests; 880 backend / 61 frontend green, both builds clean.

### Fixed
- **The ЗУВ-copy check never ran on a whole class of real РПД — including the one the методист re-tested.** She reported the same competency still scoring «Обеспечена 90%» with no formulation warning, on an item whose «должен знать» line is indicator ОПК-4.1 with its verb prefix («Знает и понимает») removed — the exact defect the check was built for, and the exact pair its own unit suite passes on.

  Root cause was the feed, not the detector. `reviewSyllabus` passed `parsed.competencies.flatMap(c => c.indicators)` — nested indicators only — and `findCopiedOutcomeFormulations` returns `[]` on an empty list. A real КНИТУ РПД lists its competencies **flat at the indicator level** (ОПК-4.1, ОПК-4.3, with no parent «ОПК-4» row carrying its own formulation), so the parser emits them as top-level `competencies` with empty `indicators` arrays, the check received nothing to compare against, and silently found nothing while the coverage pass scored the copied ЗУВ green. Her screenshot shows the tell plainly: the indicator card and the ЗНАТЬ card carry **identical citations** (same two §6 практ. topics, same §8 СРС topic) — one sentence scored twice as two separate requirements.

  Now compares against competencies **and** their indicators (`services/syllabusReview.ts`), so the check no longer depends on a parse shape half the real documents don't produce. Side effect worth noting: the РПД-студия path (caller-supplied competencies, no indicators) was previously documented as always-empty and is now covered too. The finding names its source by **code shape** rather than parse position — ФГОС numbers indicators «ОПК-4.1» and competencies «ОПК-4», so a flattened ОПК-4.1 is still called «индикатор», which is what the reader expects.

  Regression test added at the level the bug actually lived (`services/syllabusReview.test.ts`) — mocked parse reproducing her flat-competency shape, **verified to fail against the old feed and pass against the fix**. A unit test on the detector could never have caught this: that function was correct the whole time, which is why its green suite was misleading.

  **Also fixed: the warning now renders on the item card.** Her screenshot showed the second half of the problem — even had the check fired, the card itself read a clean green «Обеспечена 90%» while the only caveat sat in a block further up the page. The flagged ЗУВ card now carries a «Повтор формулировки» chip beside the status badge, a warning-tinted left border, and the copied-from text plus recommendation inline. The coverage status is deliberately **left untouched**: delivery and wording are independent questions, and folding one into the other would make covered/partial/missing mean two things at once — so the green stays, it just can no longer be read without the caveat next to it.

  The standalone block is now the overflow view only, for findings whose ЗУВ line has no card to sit on. That case is real, not theoretical: `buildRequirements` caps the scored list at `MAX_REQUIREMENTS = 40` and appends Знать/Уметь/Владеть *after* competencies and indicators, so on a large РПД (~41 requirements is typical) the lines most likely to be copied are the first to fall outside the cap — parsed and flagged, but never individually scored. Without the fallback they would have vanished the moment findings moved inline.

  First React render tests in the repo ([SyllabusReviewReport.test.tsx](frontend/src/components/curriculum/SyllabusReviewReport.test.tsx)) — the item↔finding lookup is keyed on `kind::title`, and any drift between the two sides yields no warning and no error, i.e. silently the original bug again. Verified by deliberately drifting the key and confirming three tests go red. Two things caught while writing them, worth recording: vitest runs `globals: false`, so Testing Library's automatic `afterEach` cleanup never registers and renders leak between tests (fixed locally with an explicit `afterEach(cleanup)` rather than changing the shared config); and `tsc` flagged a `ref` field in the test fixtures that isn't part of `SyllabusCoverageItem` at all — the suite was green while the fixture was wrong.

  **Still open, raised by the same message:** she also asked for a check of *смысл/формулировка* — whether the ЗУВ wording genuinely conveys the indicator's meaning and has a semantic tie to the discipline, not merely that it isn't a literal copy. The copy detector is a crude proxy; a reworded-but-empty ЗУВ still passes it. Agreed this warrants its own LLM pass (a real judgement, unlike the deterministic copy question). Folded into that same piece of work: copied ЗУВ items are still counted as their own requirements in the covered/partial/missing totals, so a РПД with this defect overstates its own coverage — two identical texts resolving to identical citations is a cheap deterministic duplicate signal.

### Changed
- **Dead-code cleanup — Phase 1 (unused imports/locals, zero behavioural change).** Removed 18 unused imports/locals/params surfaced by `tsc --noUnusedLocals --noUnusedParameters` (currently off in both tsconfigs, so these slipped past the normal build): backend — `slide` binding in `db/queries/presentations.ts`, `UnauthorizedError` import in `middleware/authenticate.ts`, unused `institution` binding in `lti.integration.test.ts` (kept the setup call for its side effects), `loginInitUrl` import in `routes/lti.ts`, `failFosDocument`/`failLongReview`/`CHARS_PER_TOKEN` in the FOS/long-review services, `ValidationError` import in `methodist/checks.test.ts`, `MAX_SLIDE_COUNT` import in `services/presentations.ts`, and the unused trailing `teacherId` param on `persistTopology` (→ `_teacherId`); frontend — `setNeedRefresh`, an unused `disciplineById` memo in `CurriculumGraph`, `updateInstitutionContract`/`OutcomeDelivery`/`useMemo` imports, the dead `Delta` badge component in `Dashboard`, a leftover `normText` in `Grading`'s `findHighlight` (superseded by the `norm` walk), and an unused `name` callback param in `RpdMonitor` (→ `_name`). Both workspaces typecheck clean; changed-file tests pass. Follow-ups (dead exports/files, cross-boundary type duplication, enabling `noUnusedLocals` in CI) tracked separately.
- **Dead-code cleanup — Phase 2 (unreferenced exports and modules, zero behavioural change).** Cross-referenced every exported symbol against the rest of the tree to find code nothing imports. Deleted 4 whole files never imported anywhere: `middleware/requireUnitRole.ts` (superseded by `requireDomain.ts`/`requireDomainOnUnitTypes` during the Phase 2/3 domain-access work — `CLAUDE.md`'s architecture doc and file tree updated to match), `lib/password.ts` (`hashPassword`/`verifyPassword` wrapper that was never adopted — every real call site uses `bcryptjs` directly), and the frontend's unused `ResearchPartners.tsx` / `usePresentations.ts`. Removed 27 further unreferenced exports (functions/consts/interfaces used nowhere, including their own file) across `db/queries/{criteria,ltiLineItems,orgUnits,profstandards,programPlacementReviews,sharedRag,teachers}.ts`, `errors/AppError.ts` (`AIServiceError`), `services/{documentExtractor,llm/registry,lti,marketingEmails,orgScope}.ts`, and frontend `api/{brs,courses,curriculum,grading,methodist,rpdMonitor}.ts` / `data/{helpArticles,helpVideos}.ts` / `hooks/{useAuth,useGrading}.ts`. One cascade caught and fixed: removing `useGrade` (a hook nobody called — the live grading flow uses the async `startGradeJob`/`getGradeJob` job pattern) left `api/grading.ts`'s synchronous `gradeSubmission` itself unreferenced, so that went too; its backend route (`POST /api/grading/grade`) was deliberately left alone since deleting an API endpoint is a different risk class (unknown external callers) from deleting dead internal code. Every removal was grep-verified against the whole repo first, including two near-misses caught before editing: `AxiosRequestConfig` in `api/client.ts` is a `declare module 'axios'` augmentation (used via the `skipErrorToast` property, not the type name) and was kept; `TeacherRoleScope` in `orgScope.ts` was still used locally even after its co-imported `loadRoleScopes` function was removed, and got re-added after an over-eager first pass dropped it. `tsc --noUnusedLocals` clean, both workspaces typecheck clean, full suites pass (861 backend / 50 frontend). Follow-up: cross-boundary type duplication (the same shape hand-copied across `services/*` ↔ `api/*` ↔ `shared/types.ts`, ~36 clusters) and enabling `noUnusedLocals`/`knip` in CI remain open.
- **Dead-code cleanup — Phase 3 (cross-boundary type deduplication, zero behavioural change).** The same interface shape was routinely hand-copied across `backend/src/{db/queries,services}/*.ts` and `frontend/src/api/*.ts` instead of living once in `shared/types.ts` — a full-body scan (not just name matching) found 41 interfaces that were byte-for-byte identical on both sides (formatting/comment noise aside) across 13 feature areas: activation funnel, grading student summaries, audit log, institution contracts, leadership dashboard, payments, policy memos, program pickers, shared RAG, capacity model, cohort analytics, doc chat, feedback library, grading response, learning loop, LTI roster, provider ceilings, and the РПД monitor overview (8 interfaces alone, including one — `RpdOverview` — that only *looked* different because of a JSDoc block my comparison script initially mis-normalized). Consolidated all 41 into `shared/types.ts`, with the backend/frontend copies replaced by an import (plus an `export type { X } from '...'` re-export shim in the ~14 files other code still imported the type from directly, so no call site had to change its import path). Deliberately **excluded** `PublishedAssignmentWithCounts` despite its own body matching — its backend and frontend versions `extend` two *different*, non-shared base types (`PublishedAssignmentRow` vs `PublishedAssignment`), so centralizing just the child would have broken the backend build; caught by the first (reverted) migration attempt actually failing to compile, not by inspection. Also caught and fixed two other real bugs in the first migration attempt before it landed: the import-insertion regex matched any line starting with `import`, which spliced a new import into the *middle* of an existing multi-line brace import wherever one was last in a file — rewritten to match whole `import ... from '...'` statements; and the relative path to `shared/types.ts` was hardcoded assuming uniform depth, which silently broke every `db/queries/*.ts` file (one directory deeper than `services/*.ts`) — replaced with `path.relative` computed per file. Both mistakes were caught by `tsc`/`noUnusedLocals` before landing, not shipped. `tsc --noUnusedLocals` clean, both workspaces typecheck **and build** clean, full suites pass (861 backend / 50 frontend, unchanged counts — pure type-location refactor). Follow-up, deliberately not touched: ~18 `*Row`-suffixed backend types with a differently-named frontend/shared counterpart (e.g. `AuditRow`↔`AuditEntry`, `FgosStandardRow`↔`FgosStandard`) — this codebase has an explicit DB-row→domain-object mapper convention (`toCriterion()`, `toReview()`, …), so a `Row` type is often *meant* to differ from its public counterpart (extra columns, `Date` vs `string`, snake_case); each of the ~18 needs a real per-pair semantic check, not a mechanical merge. Enabling `noUnusedLocals`/`knip` in CI also still open.
- **Dead-code cleanup — Phase 0 (guardrail, retroactive).** Flipped `noUnusedLocals`/`noUnusedParameters` on in both tsconfigs (`backend/tsconfig.json`, `frontend/tsconfig.json` — frontend's were explicitly `false`) now that Phases 1–3 left both workspaces at zero violations. No CI changes needed: `ci.yml` already runs `npm run typecheck --workspace=backend` and `npm run build --workspace=frontend` (`tsc && vite build`) on every push, so both flags are enforced from here on with zero new tooling — a future unused import/local now fails CI instead of silently accumulating. `knip`/`jscpd` for unused-export and duplication detection remain a manual `npx` run (`npm i -D knip jscpd` then `npx knip` / `npx jscpd backend/src frontend/src shared --min-lines 8`); wiring those into CI is left as a follow-up since it needs a dependency install this environment couldn't perform.

### Added
- **Two more real-РПД bugs found by user testing on the same day, both fixed.**

  **СРС/КСР false-missing on comma-joined instrument names.** `mentions()` required every token of an instrument's name to appear together in the target phrase — «Доклад, сообщение» tokenizes to [доклад, сообщение], and «Подготовка доклада» only has «доклад», so it failed even though it's obviously prep for that instrument. The comma there names one assessment genre two interchangeable ways (a normal РПД convention), not two co-required concepts — same for «Деловая и/или ролевая игра», whose «и/или» is explicit OR. `mentions()` now splits on comma/`/`/«и/или»/«или» into alternatives and matches if ANY alternative's tokens are fully contained — [assessmentLinkage.ts](backend/src/services/assessmentLinkage.ts). Tests added reproducing the exact reported case.

  **МТО (§12) false "раздел пуст" — same truncation bug as §9's, in a sibling file I hadn't touched.** `reviewMto` sliced `documentText.slice(0, 20000)` from the start; §12 «Материально-техническое обеспечение» sits near the end of a real РПД (after §9-11), so a flat window starting from page 1 routinely never reached it. Confirmed against a real 14-page document whose §12 clearly listed ABBYY FineReader/MS Office/7-Zip/etc. — the check reported "не называет ни оборудования, ни ПО" because the model was never shown that text. Fixed the same way: [mtoReview.ts](backend/src/services/mtoReview.ts) now calls `selectRelevantSections` (the same reusable helper the §9 fix extended) with its own heading set (§12 first, then labs/practicals for the isHandsOn/cross-check logic), budget bumped 20000 → 40000. New `mtoReview.test.ts` (first test file for this service) reproduces the shape and asserts §12 survives.

  Both bugs were invisible to the existing unit test suites because those only test the deterministic matching/finding logic with hand-built short inputs — neither exercised a document long enough to hit the truncation boundary. Worth treating as a standing lesson: every LLM-facing extraction pass in this codebase that reads a REAL uploaded document (not a short caller-supplied string) needs to reason about where in the document its target section typically falls, not just how large a "safe" budget looks in isolation.
- **«Связка оценочного средства» — fixed a systematic false-positive: §9 was routinely truncated away before the model ever saw it.** Found via a real РПД the user tested — every оценочное средство was coming back "missing from п.9" even when п.9 genuinely listed it with points. Root cause: `parseAssessmentLinkage` sliced the document text `text.slice(0, 14000)` from the START — fine for a short document, but §9 (БРС) routinely sits past page 10 of a real multi-page РПД (confirmed against the reported document: §9 was on page 11 of 14), so a flat 14000-char window covering roughly the first 5 pages never reached it. Every instrument's `found.brs` came back null not because the linkage was broken, but because §9's text was never in the prompt — the exact same failure mode `fgosExtractor.ts`'s header already documents for a late-document appendix.

  Fixed by reusing `documentReview.ts`'s `selectRelevantSections` (heading-anchored section packing, already proven for the §5-§8/§12 coverage check) instead of a naive slice — `selectRelevantSections` now takes optional `headings`/`priority` params (defaults preserve every existing caller's behaviour unchanged) so `assessmentLinkage.ts` can hunt for its own four sections (§4's structure table, §8 СРС, §8.1 КСР, §9 rating-system) regardless of where they fall in the document, instead of just grabbing whatever's first. Bumped the budget to 40000 chars (matches `documentReview.ts`'s own) now that it needs to fit four sections instead of one contiguous block. New regression test reproduces the exact shape (§4 near the top, ~30k chars of filler, §9 near the end — past the old cutoff) and asserts §9's text survives into the prompt.

  **Separately raised, not yet resolved:** whether an aggregate-style §9 table (points budgeted by control-point TYPE — «Лабораторная работа»/«Контрольная работа»/«Экзамен» — rather than one line per named оценочное средство) should satisfy the linkage check at all, or whether it's a legitimate gap the methodist wants flagged. Sent to her for a ruling before touching the matching logic — a wrong guess either way means either muting real errors or false-positiving on a common, possibly-correct БРС table style.
- **ПК ↔ профстандарт/ОТФ traceability + copy-check (methodist feedback item 3).** Third item from her review: «В учебном плане нужно сделать связь формулировки ПК-компетенции с выбранным ПРОФСТАНДАРТОМ... на основании выбранной ОТФ формулирует ПК-компетенции и индикаторы. Опять же, эти формулировки не должны быть идентичными.» Same defect class as the ЗУВ-copy fix, one level up: ПК competencies (unlike УК/ОПК, which come verbatim from the ФГОС) are institution-authored from a профстандарт's ОТФ (обобщённая трудовая функция) at the qualification level matching the programme — and neither the traceability nor the copy-check existed before.

  New migration 115: `profstandards`/`profstandard_otf` (federal reference data, admin-curated, draft→published — mirrors the ФГОС registry exactly), `program_competencies.profstandard_otf_id` (nullable link, opposite invariant from `fgos_competency_id`: УК/ОПК SHOULD be verbatim, ПК must NOT be), and `program_competency_indicators` (ПК-1.1/.2/.3 — no indicator concept existed on ПК before this). `services/profstandardExtractor.ts` (LLM extraction from an uploaded профстандарт document, mirrors `fgosExtractor.ts`) + new `/admin/profstandards` registry page. `routes/programs.ts`'s new `GET /:id/profstandard-options` resolves the programme's own профстандарты (via the existing ФГОС registry lookup) and flags every ОТФ for whether its «требования к образованию» matches the programme's level — reuses `fgosMatch.ts`'s already-tested `inferFgosLevel`, no new level-parsing logic. Конструктор's competency row gets a collapsible ОТФ-picker sub-panel for ПК codes only (профстандарт → ОТФ cascading select, read-only ОТФ text shown alongside so the author formulates while looking at the source, level-mismatch warning, indicator editor) — УК/ОПК rows are untouched.

  New `services/pkFormulation.ts` — same deterministic, no-LLM containment check as `outcomeFormulation.ts`, comparing a ПК title/indicator against its linked ОТФ's wording. Promoted the shared `tokenContainment` helper into `lib/ruText.ts` (now a two-consumer shared primitive) rather than duplicating it a second time. Wired into `programAnalysis.ts`'s `analyzeProgram()` (surfaces in Кабинет методиста's «Проверка ОП» automatically — that tab already reuses the engine wholesale) and inline into `saveCompetencies`'s response, so a warning appears under the offending ПК row on every save without a second round-trip. 5 new unit tests for the copy-check (a real ОТФ example from her screenshot as the primary case), 4 for the extractor. Verified: both typechecks clean, 853 backend + 46 frontend tests pass; browser verification of the new pages was blocked by a tool-side issue in this session, not re-attempted.
- **«Связка оценочного средства» — new п.4 ↔ СРС ↔ КСР ↔ п.9 check (methodist feedback).** Second item from the same методист's review: «Должна быть связка оценочного средства, написанного в п.4 РПД, с формой СРС, формой КСР, п.9 и ФОС — если в п.4 есть «ДОКЛАД», то в СРС должно быть «ПОДГОТОВКА ДОКЛАДА», в КСР «ЗАСЛУШИВАНИЕ ДОКЛАДА», в п.9 «ДОКЛАД» с баллами, в ФОС — доклад.» An оценочное средство named in §4's last column is a promise the rest of the РПД has to keep: the student needs time to prepare it (СРС), the teacher needs to be scheduled to assess it (КСР), and it has to carry points in the БРС (§9). A name appearing in §4 and nowhere else is an assessment that exists only on paper — and nothing checked for it before.

  New `services/assessmentLinkage.ts`, registered as a fifth entry in Кабинет методиста's check registry (`checks.ts` — the extension path that registry was built for; no migration, the result travels inline on the run row like syllabus-review since it has no dedicated table). Same split as the ЗУВ check that shipped alongside it: the **extraction** is an LLM pass (reading §4's table, СРС/КСР forms and §9 checkpoints out of unstructured prose is what a model is for, at temperature 0 so findings don't jump between runs on an unchanged document), the **linkage** is deterministic code — whether «Доклад» is present in «Подготовка доклада» is a measurable string question. Reuses the Russian stemmer built for the ЗУВ check, now extracted to `lib/ruText.ts` since a second consumer appeared.

  Three judgement calls worth recording:
  - **ФОС was, at ship time, deliberately NOT checked** — no upload path existed for it, so claiming to have verified it would've been a lie. Every finding, and the summary even on a clean pass, carried an explicit "check the ФОС yourself" reminder instead, and the UI drew the ФОС link as permanently unverified rather than green. *(Superseded below — a real upload path now exists, and the ФОС link is checked for real when one has been attached.)*
  - **Экзамен/зачёт are checked against §9 only.** They're промежуточная аттестация: requiring «Заслушивание экзамена» in КСР would fire a false finding on every correctly written РПД in the institution.
  - **A §9 entry with no points is half a link.** The instrument is named but nothing rides on it, so that's flagged separately from outright absence.

  Findings render as the four links drawn explicitly per instrument (п.4 → СРС → КСР → п.9 → ФОС), each showing the phrase that satisfied it, so the методист sees *which* link is broken at a glance and can verify the match rather than trust an assertion. 12 new unit tests over the deterministic half, built on her own «ДОКЛАД» worked example.
- **ФОС can now actually be uploaded and checked, and «Отдельный файл» runs every check «Проверка дисциплины» structurally can.** Two gaps closed after a методист asked where the new item shows up and why ФОС wasn't checked.

  New `program_documents` kind `'fos'` (no migration — `kind` is a plain TEXT column, no CHECK constraint) — a методист/РОП attaches an institution-filed ФОС per discipline the same way a working programme is attached: `POST /:id/documents` with `kind=fos`, one CURRENT file per discipline, re-upload supersedes rather than deletes (`findFosForDiscipline`/`supersedeFosForDiscipline`, mirroring the working-programme pair rather than generalising it — ~10 existing call sites made that not worth the risk). Deliberately **not** the same thing as `fos_documents` (Feature X's AI-drafted ФОС tied to a personal course) — this is the institution's real filed document. `services/assessmentLinkage.ts`'s `checkAssessmentLinkage` gained an optional `fosText` param: when a ФОС is attached, its text joins the search the same way СРС/КСР/§9 already are, and the result carries `fos_available` so the report can tell "checked and failed" apart from "never verified" — the two must never be conflated. `AssessmentLinkageReport.tsx`'s ФОС chip now reads real ok/missing state when `fos_available`, falling back to the old unverified chip only when nothing was uploaded.

  «Отдельный файл» (ad-hoc, no programme target) previously ran only the §5–§8 syllabus check; new `services/methodist/adHocChecks.ts` runs it plus МТО and the new связка check — the three of «Проверка дисциплины»'s five checks that don't need real programme data (МТО's cross-discipline suggestion sub-feature silently no-ops with no siblings; связка runs with no ФОС text by default, same as any discipline that hasn't had one uploaded). Coverage and placement stay structurally impossible ad-hoc and are documented as such in the file's header rather than faked: coverage needs a declared competency list from `program_disciplines`/`program.competencies` that doesn't exist for a freestanding file, and placement compares against the real учебный план semester order and sibling disciplines' own §2 — there's nothing to compare against. `POST /api/methodist/ad-hoc-review` now takes a `checks` param and returns `{checks: CheckOutcome[]}` (was a single `SyllabusReview`); the tab gained a checklist (all three selected by default) instead of one button.

**Same day: ФОС can be attached at the ad-hoc stage too.** «Отдельный файл» is mainly used to vet a new РПД before it's published to the university site or attached to an ОП — at that point there's no discipline row anywhere to hang a `program_documents` upload on. New optional `fos_file` field on `POST /api/methodist/ad-hoc-review` (multer `uploadFields`, same MIME/magic-byte checks as the main file): its extracted text is threaded through `runAdHocChecks`'s new `AdHocCheckOptions.fosText` straight into `checkAssessmentLinkage`, checked for real for that one run — nothing persisted, since the discipline doesn't exist yet to persist it against. The `linkage` checkbox in the tab now reveals a small optional ФОС picker when selected. This is a real gap the earlier ad-hoc-parity work left open: running every check ad-hoc doesn't help the ФОС link specifically unless there's also a way to hand it a ФОС. Verified: both typechecks clean, 843 backend + 46 frontend tests pass, page loads without a console error (no login credentials available to verify against real seeded data).
- **«Соответствие РПД компетенциям» — «Знать/Уметь/Владеть» formulation check (methodist feedback).** A методист reviewing a real РПД found the check scoring ЗУВ items at 100% «Обеспечена» when they were the competency indicators copy-pasted verbatim — indicator ОПК-4.1 «Знает и понимает сущность технологических процессов производства кулинарной продукции…» and the «должен знать» line beneath it were the same sentence minus the verb. Her rule, stated precisely: a ЗУВ formulation must convey the indicator's **meaning** through the discipline's own content; being identical to it is a defect, not a pass.

  New `services/outcomeFormulation.ts` flags every ЗУВ line that merely restates a declared indicator. Two deliberate design calls, both documented in the file's header:

  - **Deterministic, no LLM call.** "Is this text a copy of that text" is a measurable string question, not a judgement — doing it in code makes it free, instant, reproducible run-to-run, and impossible to hallucinate. (The coverage scoring itself is a real judgement and rightly stays with the model.) Token-set *containment* rather than a symmetric similarity, because the defect is asymmetric: the ЗУВ line is the indicator with its verb prefix chopped off, so it's a near-subset, and a symmetric score would be dragged down by the indicator's extra words. A crude Russian suffix stripper collapses inflections — lifting a phrase into a «Знать:» list shifts its case endings, and without stemming that trivially-reworded copy (the «почти дословно» half of the report) scores below threshold while only the exact copy is caught.
  - **A separate findings list, not a demotion of the coverage status.** "Does the content deliver this requirement?" and "is this requirement's wording copy-pasted?" are independent questions — a copied ЗУВ can still be genuinely delivered. Folding one into the other would make the covered/partial/missing counts mean two things at once. Same shape as the §2/§12 checks, which already carry their own findings lists alongside their verdicts.

  The verdict line does now refuse to report coverage without the formulation result: a РПД whose content delivers copy-pasted ЗУВ items previously read as «полностью обеспечивает», which is exactly the false all-clear that was reported. The report renders the findings **above** the coverage breakdown, showing the ЗУВ line and the indicator in full one above the other, so the reader sees the duplication themselves instead of trusting a similarity number — the same "show the evidence" contract the coverage citations follow. 12 new unit tests, with the reported ОПК-4.1 pair as the primary case (if that pair ever stops being flagged, the check has regressed to the reported bug) plus a genuine discipline-specific reformulation that must stay unflagged.
- **Кабинет методиста (TODO Feature AM) — Phase 3 complete: ad-hoc file-upload target.** Last piece of the feature: a «Отдельный файл» tab + `POST /api/methodist/ad-hoc-review` for a РПД that isn't attached to any programme at all (arrived by email, still being drafted) — the one target with no `getProgramAccessScope` to check, gated purely on `methodist_access`. Accepts either an uploaded file (same MIME allowlist/magic-byte verification as every other upload path in this codebase — `uploadConfig` + `verifyFileContent`) or pasted text; extracts text via the existing `services/documentExtractor.ts` for the file case, then calls `reviewSyllabus` directly with no competencies/goals — the parser extracts them from the document's own declared sections, the same behaviour the raw-text path on `routes/curriculum.ts`'s syllabus-review already has. Nothing is persisted anywhere — not even a run row, since a single check with no programme/discipline target has nothing worth polling for; the result lives only in the tab's client state. Frontend: `reviewAdHocSyllabus` (`api/methodist.ts`, `FormData` upload mirroring `api/mySyllabi.ts`'s `submitSyllabusFile`), a file/text mode toggle, and the same `SyllabusReviewReport` every other syllabus check in the app renders through — no new report component needed.

  This closes out TODO Feature AM (Кабинет методиста) end to end: Phase 0 (de-anchored checks from personal-course ownership), Phase 1 (shell + discipline/overlap tabs + МУМЦ access gate), Phase 2 (async pg-boss check runs), Phase 3 (Очередь triage tab, «Проверка ОП» whole-programme analysis, ad-hoc file target, layout redesign). Verified: both typechecks clean, 807 backend + 46 frontend tests pass, page loads without a console error.

  **Same-day tweak:** «Отдельный файл» moved to the first tab and the default landing view (was Очередь) — no other state to load, so it's the fastest path from opening the page to actually checking something.
- **Кабинет методиста (TODO Feature AM) — Phase 3: «Проверка ОП» whole-programme analysis.** New tab reuses `services/programAnalysis.ts` wholesale — the exact same `analyzeProgram`/`getAnalysis`/`downloadAnalysisPdf` endpoints the programme detail page's Report tab already calls, nothing new server-side. Cached analysis loads on tab open; a button re-runs it (~2–3 min, synchronous, same shape as the already-shipped overlap check).

  Made possible by extracting `InstitutionProgramDetail.tsx`'s `Report` renderer's small reusable atoms (`scoreColor`, `Stat`, `SectionLabel`, `OutcomeDeliveryCard`, `GapColumn`, `EdgeCard`) into new `components/curriculum/ProgramAnalysisSummary.tsx` — both pages now import them instead of each owning a copy. The file also gained a genuinely new composite, `ProgramAnalysisSummary`, which is deliberately **not** a byte-identical port of `Report`: it keeps score/verdict/warnings/outcome-delivery/sequencing-inversions/gaps/load — exactly what a методист is checking for — and drops the competency-progression heatmap table and the dependency-layer pathway graph, both wide, editing-adjacent visualisations that don't fit a ~700px results column and belong more to the РОП's authoring workspace (`/programs/:id`) than a методист's read-only check. `InstitutionProgramDetail.tsx` itself is unchanged in behaviour, only rewired to import the shared atoms — 2305 → 2203 lines.

  Verified: both typechecks clean, 807 backend + 46 frontend tests pass, `/institution/methodist` and `/programs` both load without a console error.
- **Кабинет методиста (TODO Feature AM) — Phase 3: Очередь triage tab + layout redesign.** Two pieces shipped the same day.

  **Layout redesign (`ui-ux-pro-max` skill).** The page was one long stacked column — programme picker, then a scrollable checklist, then the run button at the bottom, then the report further below that — so running a check meant scrolling past the whole list to reach the button, then scrolling again for results. Rebuilt on the same split-panel pattern `Grading.tsx` already uses in production: a fixed-width (340px) controls column that's always visible, and an independently-scrolling results column, each with its own `overflow-y-auto`. The programme picker and tab switcher moved into a compact header row. On mobile the two columns become tab-switched views (Настройка/Результат — same `MobileSubtabs` shape as Grading's Работа/Результат), auto-switching to Результат the moment a run starts. Added an empty-state placeholder in the results panel before the first run.

  **Очередь tab (cross-programme triage), now the default landing tab.** New `GET /api/methodist/queue` reuses `services/umcDashboard.ts`'s aggregation (Feature V) wholesale — calls the exact same `getUmcDashboard()` Готовность УМК calls — but under a different gate: `requireDomainOnUnitTypes('curriculum', 'view', METHODIST_UNIT_TYPES)` instead of `requireDomain('umu', 'view')`. Deliberately not a widened gate on the existing `/institution/umc-dashboard` route — `docs/ACCESS-MATRIX.md` documents "Готовность УМК строго УМУ + РУМЦ" as a real, incident-driven exclusion; this is a separate screen for a different audience (adds МУМЦ, who has `methodist_access` but not `umu_access`) reading the same signals, not a change to that invariant. Frontend's new `QueueTab` filters the full readiness matrix down to only "needs attention" rows (`queueUrgency`: no РПД uploaded → uploaded but never reviewed → reviewed with under 50% coverage, in that priority order) instead of reproducing Готовность УМК's exhaustive matrix — triage, not a duplicate dashboard. Clicking a row jumps straight into «Проверка дисциплины» with that programme+discipline preselected (required lifting `disciplineId` state from `DisciplineChecksTab` up to the page root). 5 new frontend unit tests (`InstitutionMethodist.test.ts`) pin the urgency classification, including the null-coverage-counts-as-low edge case.

  Verified: both typechecks clean, 807 backend + 46 frontend tests pass, page loads without a console error (one red herring along the way — a stale Vite dependency cache from repeated dev-server restarts this session threw an unrelated React hook-call crash on every route including `/`; `rm -rf node_modules/.vite` fixed it, confirming it long predated and was unrelated to this change).

  **Not done yet:** «Проверка ОП» (whole-programme analysis — blocked on extracting `InstitutionProgramDetail.tsx`'s ~330-line `Report` renderer into `components/curriculum/`, not yet done) and an ad-hoc file-upload target for a РПД not yet attached to a programme.
- **Кабинет методиста (TODO Feature AM) — Phase 2: async check runs (pg-boss).** «Проверка дисциплины» ran its up-to-4 checks as separate synchronous mutations in Phase 1 — fine for one check, but "run all four" meant four HTTP sockets each held open on an independent LLM call. New `services/methodist/checks.ts`: a registry where each of the four checks is a thin adapter onto the existing service (`reviewSyllabus`, `reviewDocumentCoverage`, `reviewPlacement` — including the same best-effort topology-graph densification the programme-page route performs, so a run from here behaves identically to one from `/institution/programs/:id`, not a lesser version — and `reviewMto`). Every adapter persists to the SAME table the equivalent programme-page route already writes (`program_document_reviews`/`program_placement_reviews`/`program_mto_reviews`) — Кабинет методиста never forks a parallel result store; `syllabus` is the one exception, stored inline on the run row since it has no dedicated table anywhere in the codebase. `runCheck` converts a thrown error into a per-check `{status:'error'}` outcome instead of aborting the whole batch — a discipline missing competency codes still returns the other 3 results — except `NotFoundError` on a bad target, which aborts the run since every check shares one target.

  New `routes/methodist.ts`: `POST /runs` validates the target + access via `loadReadableDiscipline` *before* enqueueing (a bad id 403/404s immediately rather than queuing a run guaranteed to fail), `GET /runs/:id` polls, `GET /runs` lists recent history. Runs go through `services/jobQueue.ts` (pg-boss) via new `services/methodist/runWorker.ts` (mirrors `fosWorker.ts`) — up to 4 independent LLM calls run in parallel, bounded by the slowest one. New additive migration 114 (`methodist_runs`: target refs + status + `checks: CheckOutcome[]`, each entry a `result_id` pointer into the existing tables, not duplicated content).

  Frontend: `InstitutionMethodist.tsx`'s «Проверка дисциплины» tab now enqueues one run and polls it (`api/methodist.ts`, same poll-loop shape `FosStudio.tsx` already uses — fetch immediately, `setTimeout` delay between polls, a ref-based cancellation guard so a second click can't let a stale poll clobber a newer run's results), then re-fetches each `ok` outcome's full result from its table by `result_id` (reusing the already-existing `getDisciplineReviews`/`getPlacementReviews`/`getMtoReviews` — no new GET-by-id routes needed) and renders a per-check error banner for any `status:'error'` outcome instead of silently dropping it.

  5 new backend tests (`checks.test.ts`) pin the per-check error-isolation contract (a `ValidationError` becomes a per-check error outcome; a `NotFoundError` propagates and aborts the run; one failing check in a batch doesn't block the others). Verified: backend typecheck clean, 807 tests pass, frontend typecheck clean, `/institution/methodist` loads without a console error.

  **Same-day layout redesign (ui-ux-pro-max):** the page was one long stacked column — programme picker, then a scrollable discipline/checks list, then the run button at the bottom, then the report further below that — so running a check meant scrolling past the whole list to reach the button, then scrolling again to see results. Rebuilt on the same split-panel pattern `Grading.tsx` already uses in production: a fixed-width (340px) controls column (picker, checklist, run button — always visible, no scrolling past it) and an independently-scrolling results column, each with its own `overflow-y-auto` so neither pushes the other off screen. The programme picker and the two top-level tabs (Проверка дисциплины / Пересечение содержания) moved into a compact header row instead of eating vertical space in the body. On mobile the two columns become tab-switched views (Настройка/Результат, same `MobileSubtabs` shape as Grading's Работа/Результат), auto-switching to Результат the moment a run starts so the loading state is never scrolled out of view. Added an empty-state placeholder (icon + hint) in the results panel before the first run, replacing a blank column. Verified: frontend typecheck clean; could not screenshot against real data — no test credentials for a методист/УМУ account in this environment.

  **Same-day UX fix (caught in review, not by a user):** the discipline pickers gave no indication of which disciplines actually had a working programme document attached — a методист could select 3 disciplines for overlap analysis with none of them documented, click «Найти дублирование», and if fewer than 2 ended up with any content the request would throw before ever producing a result; the only feedback was a global toast easy to miss, with nothing left on screen afterward. Both pickers now derive a `disciplinesWithRpd` set from `program.documents` (already returned by `GET /institution/programs/:id`, no new endpoint) and show it inline — a green «РПД загружена» / muted «нет РПД» chip per row in the overlap multi-select, an inline warning box under the single-discipline dropdown, and an option-label suffix. `run()` in both tabs now pre-empts a doomed request client-side with a clear message instead of letting it round-trip and fail silently, and the overlap tab's mutation gained an `onError` that renders a banner that stays on screen (not just the transient global toast) until the next attempt.
- **Кабинет методиста (TODO Feature AM) — Phase 0 slice 1: `syllabus-review` no longer requires a personal course.** Prompted by user testing with a real методист: their role grants were correct, but every syllabus check lived behind `resolveCourseText(courseId, teacherId)` — course ownership — so they had to find-or-create a personal предмет via `POST /programs/:id/disciplines/:disciplineId/studio-course` just to run a check, burning their plan's `maxCourses` cap mid-audit and leaving the work invisible to everyone else. New `services/methodist/target.ts`'s `resolveProgramDisciplineText` resolves `{program_id, discipline_id}` straight from `program_documents`/`program_disciplines` via the existing `findWorkingProgrammeForDiscipline`, gated on `getProgramAccessScope`/`canReadProgram` — the same read check `/api/institution/programs` already enforces, no new access primitive. Wired into `POST /api/curriculum/syllabus-review` as a third target alongside `course_id` and raw `syllabus_text`. Backend-only — no UI yet; Phase 1 adds the Кабинет методиста shell. 5 new unit tests (`target.test.ts`).

  **Slice 2 (same day):** the overlap check (`POST /api/curriculum/overlap`, TODO Feature A3) had the identical problem one level deeper — `services/curriculumAnalysis.ts`'s `analyzeCurriculumOverlap` resolved `courseIds` itself via `findCourseById(teacherId)`, so it couldn't be pointed at a programme discipline at all. Decoupled the service from course ownership entirely: it now takes pre-resolved `{id, name, content}` items (new `OverlapItem` type) instead of `courseIds`+`teacherId`, and resolution moved up into `routes/curriculum.ts`, which builds that list from either a teacher's own courses (existing behaviour, unchanged) or `services/methodist/target.ts`'s new `resolveProgramDisciplinesText` — a batch variant that does one programme fetch + one access check for the whole comparison set, then resolves each discipline's text in parallel, skipping (not failing) any discipline with no uploaded/short РПД so a partial selection still produces a result. 5 new tests in `curriculumAnalysis.test.ts` pin the decoupled contract.

  **Scope decided, same day:** ФОС/БРС generation and `syllabus-draft` (РПД-студия authoring) stay teacher/course-anchored, permanently — a методист's role is checking/analysing what a teacher or РОП already authored, not generating or owning artifacts. De-anchoring work is scoped to checks only.

  **Phase 1 slice 1 (same day):** the Кабинет методиста shell — new `pages/institution/InstitutionMethodist.tsx` at `/institution/methodist`, one `NAV` entry in `InstitutionLayout.tsx`. Programme → discipline picker (same `GET /api/institution/programs` scope РОП Студия already uses) → runs the §5–§8 evidence-citation check via the new `reviewProgramDisciplineSyllabus`/`analyzeProgramOverlap` API client functions. Extracted the report renderer (`Results`/`ParsedReport`/`CoverageCard`/`Stat` + the `STATUS_META`/`KIND_META`/`SECTION_LABEL` tables) out of `CurriculumConformance.tsx` into a new shared `components/curriculum/SyllabusReviewReport.tsx` — both the teacher-facing page and the new методист page now render the identical report off one component instead of duplicating ~180 lines. Verified the route loads without a console error or crash (redirects correctly to `/login` when unauthenticated); full interactive verification against seeded programme/discipline/РПД data needs a real методист/УМУ login, not yet available in this environment.

  **Phase 1 slice 2 (same day):** the Кабинет методиста shell gained two things it was missing to actually replace the studio-jumping — a «Проверка дисциплины» tab that runs four checks side by side (§5–§8 coverage, competency coverage via `reviewDiscipline`, «место в структуре» via `reviewDisciplinePlacement`, МТО via `reviewDisciplineMto` — the last three were already programme-anchored in `routes/programs.ts`, never had the course-anchor problem, just weren't reachable from one screen), and a «Пересечение содержания» tab (multi-select disciplines within a programme → `analyzeProgramOverlap`). Made possible without duplicating ~470 lines of render code by extracting two more shared components: `components/curriculum/CheckPanels.tsx` (placement/MTO/competency-coverage panels, pulled out of `InstitutionProgramDetail.tsx` — 2588 → 2305 lines) and `components/curriculum/OverlapReport.tsx` (pulled out of `Curriculum.tsx` — 319 → 171 lines); both original pages now import the shared components instead of owning a private copy, so a future fix to how a panel renders lands in one place. Verified: both typechecks clean, 803 backend tests pass, `/institution/methodist` and `/curriculum` both load without a console error after the extraction.

  **МУМЦ gate fix (same day):** the slice 1 NAV entry initially gated on the `umu` domain, which turned out to exactly exclude `Методист УМЦ` (МУМЦ) — Table A gives МУМЦ only `curriculum:edit`, no `umu` grant at all. Widening МУМЦ's bundle to `umu:view` was rejected: `umu` also gates Мониторинг РПД/Готовность УМК, which `docs/ACCESS-MATRIX.md` §4 verifies as strictly УМУ+РУМЦ — that would have silently widened those too. Shipped a narrow `methodist_access` flag instead — `services/accessScope.ts`'s new `METHODIST_UNIT_TYPES = ['admin_office']` + `resolveGrantOnUnitTypes(scope, 'curriculum', 'view', METHODIST_UNIT_TYPES)`, the same derived-flag pattern already used for `criteria_access`/`org_overview_access`. Admits УМУ/РУМЦ/МУМЦ (all sit on `admin_office`, or institution-root which `resolveGrantOnUnitTypes` always admits) while excluding ЗК/РОП/РПГ/ДИ/ДЕК, whose `curriculum` grants sit on `department`/`program`/`cluster`/`division` — matches how the кабинет's checks actually gate access today (`getProgramAccessScope`, `curriculum` domain), so `umu` was never the real dependency. Wired through `routes/auth.ts` (payload), `shared/types.ts`, and — critically — all three frontend gates that had to move together: `InstitutionLayout.tsx`'s NAV filter, `Sidebar.tsx`'s «Организация» link visibility, and `App.tsx`'s `InstitutionRoute` guard. Missing the last one would have redirected МУМЦ to `/dashboard` before ever reaching the NAV filter — the same three-gate shape `criteria_access`/`org_overview_access` already established, followed exactly rather than improvised. 2 new tests in `accessScope.test.ts` (admits МУМЦ's `admin_office` grant, rejects a ЗК's `department` grant at the same level).
- **Two more deal-agnostic Track 2 items — runtime frontend config (2.2) and onprem fallback denial (2.6).** `on-prem-deployment.md` §16. New `frontend/public/config.js`, a plain classic script loaded before the app bundle, sets `window.__ISPUM_CONFIG__` so `frontend/src/lib/runtimeConfig.ts`'s `getApiBaseUrl()` can override the backend origin per deployment by editing one static file — no rebuild.
